In the \"Answer\" sheet:\n     - Cell C3: Z-score for 95% one-sided confidence = 1.645\n     - Cell C4: Latest 3-month volatility value from Gold price sheet column D (last value)\n     - Cell C5: 3-month volatility annualized = C4 * SQRT(12) (annualized from rows 3-6)\n     - If a country doesn't have 2025 total reserve data, delete it from the STEP 3 table\n   - In row 24, calculate RaR (Risk as a Ratio) = (row 22 / row 23) * 100\n     - This shows gold valuation exposure as a percentage of total reserves\n\nUse openpyxl to modify the Excel file at /root/data/test-rar.xlsx and save it to /root/output/rar_result.xlsx.\n\nKey hints:\n- Multiply log returns by 100 to express as percentage\n- For 3-month annualized: multiply 3-month volatility by SQRT(12)\n- Dates in CSV are in format like \"2025M9\" for September 2025\n- Jan-Sep 2025 = months from 2025M1 to 2025M9\n- For formulas in Excel: use INDEX+MATCH like =INDEX($B$9:$B$17,MATCH(column_header,$A:$A,0))\n- Keep only countries that have complete data (especially 2025 total reserves for STEP 3)"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":599,"cache_read_input_tokens":22161,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":599,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"055976ae-df3e-46af-bd8b-b2eb38a17fad","uuid":"0a5c91a6-bb10-4d2b-acdc-1a8bb9579aab"}

Calculate 3-month volatility in column D: STDEV of the last 3 monthly log returns\n   - Calculate 12-month volatility in column E: STDEV of the last 12 monthly log returns\n   - In the \"Answer\" sheet:\n     -
